TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种新方法LODESTAR，通过强化学习训练一个极化器，将其作为自然语言字符串插入提示中，以提升检索增强问答系统的答案准确率，并超越了基于熵选择的现有方法，在多个数据集上取得了最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强问答方法常用熵作为选择依据，认为回答概率分布熵最低的候选答案最可靠。然而，若检索到的文本误导模型，模型会自信但错误地回答，导致熵选择法失效。因此需要新的机制来提升选择过程的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者提出LODESTAR方法，利用强化学习训练一个短文本极化器（polarizer），插入到LLM的prompt中。极化器的训练无需改变模型参数，标签基于金标答案和LLM的判决离线获得。在推断时，通过比较不同候选对模型不确定性的影响以做出更优选择。

Result: 在5008个问题、五个问答数据集上，LODESTAR在所有对比的推理场景下取得了最高的均值F1（0.5339）、精确匹配（0.4136）和GPT-4o判分（0.6435），全面优于十四种先进配置，并对误导性文本的错误选择次数明显下降。

Conclusion: LODESTAR通过对输入context微干预，即强化学习生成的短文本极化器，能让冻结的LLM更好地区分正确与误导性候选，从而改进检索增强问答的表现，为无需变更模型权重、提升可靠性的问答方法提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predictive-distribution entropy makes a strong selection rule in retrieval-augmented question answering: across five QA benchmarks, keeping the candidate answer that a frozen respondent LLM produces with the lowest answer-token entropy lifts mean answer $F_1$ from 0.4769 to 0.5148 over the retriever's top-ranked passage, with no gold answers. Yet this lowest-entropy rule, which prior entropy-based selectors adopt, fails in a specific and consequential way: a misleading passage makes the respondent confidently wrong, driving its entropy down precisely where the signal looks most trustworthy. We show that the failure comes from the passage the respondent reads -- and the context that passage is read in is an input we can intervene on. We introduce LODESTAR, to our knowledge the first method to score a text intervention by the uncertainty it induces in a third-party frozen respondent, compared across one question's candidates. LODESTAR uses reinforcement learning to train, once and offline, a polarizer -- a short fixed natural-language string inserted into the respondent's prompt and never into its weights; its training labels are built offline from gold answers and two LLM judges, and inference reads neither. Evaluating every competing selector under the same frozen respondent and the same candidate pools on 5,008 questions, LODESTAR attains the highest mean $F_1$ of any inference-ready selector (0.5148 to 0.5339), the highest exact match (0.4136), and the highest GPT-4o judge score of the frozen-respondent configurations judged (0.6435); its three-seed mean wins all 70 method-by-dataset $F_1$ cells against fourteen published configurations while remaining paired-significant against every one. The gain holds both in-domain and out-of-domain, and ablating the polarizer shows it is what makes the respondent read a misleading passage less often (26.0% against 30.3%).

</details>


### [143] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spark-to-Paper系统，实现端到端自动化生成研究论文，包括文献检索、实验设计与执行、证据修正与图表生成，并集成于主流代码助手中，无需额外编排或代理。其通过分离判断与操作、实验规划与报告，提升可靠性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动化写作系统主要聚焦文本生成，难以涵盖学术论文所需的复杂流程（如实验筹划、证据审查、图像生成等）。现有解决方案普遍依赖繁琐的多代理或外部管控机制，缺乏高效和可维护性。

Method: 将论文生成流程拆解为十三个可组合技能，嵌入代码助手，实现从想法到论文的自动化。采用模型判断结合确定性操作，分离实验规划与报告，借助完整性检查、自我批判，提高长流程的可靠性，并通过编程绘图和代码重构生成可编辑矢量图。

Result: 在八个受控课题中，系统实现99.5%的文献引用有效性和96.4%的图像可编辑性。完整评审和完整性机制可将伪造检测率由单轮草稿的14%提升至92%，对抗评审精度达74%。综合性能上，单篇论文消耗11.9M tokens，费用$8.1，平均3.2小时。

Conclusion: Spark-to-Paper验证了自动化论文写作可作为集成轻量化流程实现，系统将实验证据作为论文核心，有效提升了学术生成流程的可信度与可操作性。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [144] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多项选择题（MCQ）常用于评估大语言模型，但选项顺序敏感性影响其可靠性。本文尝试消除位置影响，提出两种去偏方法，并评估其效果。结果显示，这些方法未有效提升准确率，根本问题在于不给模型看选项导致信息损失，消除位置影响并不总能带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MCQ基准混合了知识评估与对选项顺序的敏感度，不够纯粹和可靠。如何设计能真实反映模型知识的测评方法是重要问题。

Method: 作者提出并评估了两种去除位置偏差的方法：一是生成-再匹配策略（先让模型生成答案，再和选项比对匹配）；二是单独给模型评分各选项（天然无序）。此外还分析了不同配置下的表现和影响。

Result: 两种去偏法都未显著提升模型准确率。通过详细分解分析后发现，准确率瓶颈不在于匹配步骤，而是不给模型看所有选项的过程本身。只有让模型看全部选项并用匹配器才达到基线水平。

Conclusion: 完全消除选项顺序影响并不必然提升MCQ测评准确率。有时对选项顺序做循环排列反而更优。两阶段方法下无论是召回不平衡指标还是具体顺序敏感度都没显示可靠去偏效果。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>


### [145] [Benchmarking Trustworthiness of SLMs: Pre-trained vs. Compressed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11981)
*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对小型语言模型（SLM）在多方面可信度进行了系统评估，发现通过量化方法由大型可信模型压缩得到的SLM在可信度和适应性上优于从零训练的小模型，并可通过知识蒸馏进一步提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）在实际场景中的应用增加，SLM因资源效率成为热门选择。然而，SLM的可信度建设（如公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理）少有系统研究。

Method: 对比了两种SLM构建方式：一是直接训练小模型，二是将大型预训练模型通过剪枝、量化或知识蒸馏等压缩方法缩小。本文重点评估了剪枝与量化对模型可信度的影响，并进一步分析了由被验证可靠的大型模型压缩生成的SLM，以及采用知识蒸馏提升SLM可靠性的方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，量化较剪枝能更有效地保留模型的可信度。综上，与直接训练小模型相比，由可信大型模型通过量化压缩得到的SLM在信任性和适应能力上表现更优。同时通过知识蒸馏还能进一步提升其可靠性。

Conclusion: 压缩可信大型模型（采用量化并可结合知识蒸馏）是建设可信SLM的有效路径。这为实践中开发与部署可信小型语言模型提供了重要参考和方法基础。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [146] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为A-CRC-QA的后处理不确定性校准框架，用于提升大型语言模型在问答任务中的可靠性，尤其在选择性答题场景下。方法对各种不确定性打分器无硬性要求，无需额外训练，并在多个数据集上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型常生成流畅但有误的答案，因此在实际应用中，需要校准其不确定性以保证答题系统的可靠性。现有用阈值过滤答案的方法缺乏对实际错误率的统计控制，不能很好区分正确与错误预测。

Method: 作者提出A-CRC-QA框架，把有选择条件下的错误率控制转化为线性期望约束，并结合单调化的经验风险校准过程，灵感来自共形风险控制。该方法可以与多种不确定性估算器结合，无需模型再训练，关注渐近（大样本）下的风险控制。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA两个数据集实验表明，与未经校准或简单置信度过滤的方法相比，A-CRC-QA能更好平衡保留答案数量和提升已接受答案的可靠性，适用于开放式和封闭式问答。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA是一种通用、免训练的答案选择与校准方案，有效提升了LLMs在需要保证问答可信度场景下的使用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可在12种孟加拉语方言间多向翻译的神经机器翻译系统，并构建了迄今最大规模的多方言平行语料库，极大提升了低资源方言的翻译表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语有超过2.4亿使用者，存在大量地区方言，这些方言在语音、词形、词汇等方面与标准孟加拉语差异巨大，当前主流NMT架构和大模型普遍假设语言分布均匀，导致低资源方言翻译效果严重下降。因此，迫切需要适应多方言的翻译系统和高质量、多样化的语料支持。

Method: 作者提出了一个统一的多方言神经机器翻译系统，能够跨12种方言实现多向翻译，无需经由标准语中转。他们还构建了包含51,531个多方言平行句对的新数据集，并引入DoRA方法对BanglaT5模型进行微调，系统性分析方言迁移和数据规模影响，并实现模型INT8量化部署。

Result: 所提出的BanglaT5模型在BLEU和chrF++等指标上超过了NLLB-200和mBART-50，表现优异，且保持了形态一致性；数据集和方言迁移阈值分析为低资源方言适应提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 作者的系统显著提升了孟加拉语多方言翻译水平，为推动边缘方言数字包容和未来相关研究提供了数据资源和技术平台，数据集及系统已公开发布。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [148] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了偏好树优化（PTO）框架，用于迭代提升多轮、目标导向对话系统，特别适用于数据稀缺的专业领域。实验结果验证了该方法在动机性访谈领域的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮、目标导向的对话系统中，尤其是数据有限的专业领域下，当前对话系统难以进行高效、有效的长期对话。动机性访谈是一种推动行为变化的重要咨询方法，该领域急需数据高效的对话代理训练方案。

Method: 提出了偏好树优化（PTO）框架，利用带有前瞻功能的偏好树（Preference Tree with Look-Ahead）迭代生成偏好数据，辅以虚拟病人和评估器模拟对话。再结合直接偏好优化（DPO）进行模型训练，并通过前瞻模拟提升模型在长期对话中的策略和规划能力。

Result: 实验表明，使用PTO训练的模型在动机性访谈场景下的关键指标（如会话满意度和工作联盟）上持续超越了基线方法。前瞻模拟层数越深，模型规划能力和对话策略越优，结果也更稳定。

Conclusion: PTO框架可缓解目标导向对话系统中的数据稀缺问题，提升模型在专业场景中的对话能力和决策水平，尤其适用于如动机性访谈一类要求高质量对话的领域。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [149] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文梳理了NLP中与“视角”相关的主要概念，提出了这些概念的判别属性，并将它们线性组织成一个层级体系，有助于明确领域研究的方向和选择合适的视角定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，文本中的“视角”相关概念（如立场、情感、框架、论证等）繁多，但它们之间的关系尚不清楚，亟需深入的理论理解以促进相关研究。

Method: 作者首先梳理和评审了NLP中关于“视角”的各类核心概念，提炼出一组区分这些概念的重要属性，并据此将诸多视角相关概念按单一轴线性地组织为一个层级结构。

Result: 分析产出了一组判别不同视角相关概念的属性，并建立了一个能清晰区分这些概念的线性层级体系。

Conclusion: 该层级体系和属性框架，为NLP研究者在具体任务中选取合适的视角概念及其操作化方式提供了理论和方法的参考，有助于推动相关研究的系统性和有效性。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [150] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为QV-PIC的新框架，通过将文本渲染为图片并结合查询感知的缓存复用机制，提升了大模型生成的效率和准确性。新方法有效压缩了输入，同时修复过往方法在缓存复用时的信息损失与上下文不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG（Retrieval-Augmented Generation）系统在不同查询间重复前填相同的文本块，导致大量冗余计算。现有位置无关缓存（PIC）虽可复用KV对减少计算，但受到文本长度和信息损失限制，尤其在以图片形式渲染文本时效果更差。急需新方法兼顾高效与精度。

Method: 作者提出了QV-PIC，一种查询感知的双分辨率缓存复用框架。其核心做法分为两个阶段：1）离线期间，基于模型原生模板将文本渲染为图片生成高质量缓存，提升缓存一致性；2）在线期间，低分辨率覆盖全局语境，高分辨率有选择地还原与当前查询最相关的信息，实现效率与精准度的平衡。

Result: 在六项任务上，QV-PIC相比标准图片渲染PIC提升平均F1分数21.6点，与标准文本PIC表现接近，甚至超越了优化的文本PIC（高2.58 F1），同时TTFT延迟降低17.2%。相较于完全预填充，TTFT降低了83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC方法显著提升了RAG系统在效率和准确性方面的表现，解决了以图片形式复用缓存时的主要难题，为大规模推理和生成提供了更优解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [151] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAG（SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation）结构化检索架构，通过事件-实体索引高效组织文档，无需预先构建全局知识图谱，在多跳推理与结构性约束下显著提升检索与问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法依赖稠密检索，难以处理结构化约束和多跳推理。图结构方法虽可改善，但维护成本高、更新不便且语义容易碎片化。研究者希望找到无需全局图谱、又能有效处理复杂关系的结构化检索框架。

Method: SAG架构将文档分块，每块视为“语义完整的事件+相关实体”，形成隐式高阶关系（超边）。在查询时，系统以共享实体为“连接键”动态联结相关事件块，生成仅与查询相关的邻域。整个过程不分解原始语义，保持证据完整性。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue等多跳推理基准上，SAG在检索和端到端问答性能均优于其它方法，且推理链复杂度越高，优势越明显。尤以MuSiQue数据集表现显著，Recall@5达80.36%，较最强基线提升11.52个百分点。

Conclusion: SAG为支持大模型智能体持续检索和多步推理组织性知识提供了新路径，为大规模知识集成和复杂推理应用奠定基础。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [152] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一款面向印度及低中收入国家（LMIC）的临床知识检索生成系统VITA，并在健康领域的公开基准测试中表现优异，优于多款主流通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在医学基准上表现优异，但测评对象有限且以高收入国家数据为主，难以满足印度及LMIC等特定环境下的临床知识需求。

Method: VITA系统采用检索增强生成（RAG）架构，从包含疾病指南、印度抗生素耐药性、本国药典和资源受限护理规程等多源专业知识数据库中智能检索信息，并通过私有架构与语料库完成生成。以开放的HealthBench医学问答集作为评价基准，通过多版本GPT与第三方大模型进行对比与评分。

Result: 在4,023个HealthBench英文问题上，VITA在评分项上超越了GPT-5.4、o4-mini、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.6等最新通用大模型。对500个问题的最新回测显示，VITA与GPT-5.5得分接近，但VITA在加权和获胜问题数上仍具优势。VITA的准确性和完整性在中立评审下持续领先，沟通性略逊。

Conclusion: 专业定制临床RAG系统（如VITA）在临床应用场景下仍具备与前沿通用大模型竞争的能力，表明领域特异性语料是提升医学大模型实践价值的有效方式，但也存在沟通表达上的权衡。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [153] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性研究了层交错HLA大模型中的大规模激活(MA)现象，揭示了两种与模型结构紧密相关的形态：注意力层前的激活峰（PAS）和线性注意力层内持续的激活平台（ISP），分析其在不同模型、配置、数据域上的普适性及机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层交错的混合注意力大模型结构中，激活分布规律尚未被深入研究。了解MA的规律有助于揭示模型内部信息流动与变换机理，对优化结构和解释大模型行为具有重要意义。

Method: 研究者在五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域和各类开源混合模型（1.2B-397B参数）中系统分析MA，进行受控预训练实验，结合机制性分析和系统性异常检测，探究MA的生命周期及其与注意力结构的关联。

Result: 实验发现，PAS和ISP这两种MA形态在多种模型与配置下高度一致，并且其形态由完整注意力层的密度调控。输出门控对激活幅度有显著但不破坏结构的影响，不同类型门控影响有明显差异。

Conclusion: MA的两种形态代表了层交错模型普遍存在的组织规律，其产生和消除机制受注意力结构和门控控制。该研究加深了对大模型内部动力学的理解，为未来结构设计等提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [154] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出信息充裕悖论，指出大模型训练过程中上下文窗口过长，反而会降低模型内部参数化记忆对知识的内化能力，过度依赖输入上下文，导致在部分任务下性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流观点认为增加语言模型训练的上下文窗口只会带来好处，长文档训练有利于模型获得更多信息。但作者希望质疑和分析是否“越长越好”这个假设。

Method: 作者在大模型的预训练和监督微调阶段，系统性地实验了不同上下文长度对语言建模、文本理解、问答等任务的影响，并对模型的内部机制如梯度分布、注意力以及因果干预进行了深入分析。

Result: 实验发现，增加上下文窗口长度在部分任务上提升表现只到一定最优点，随后反而表现下滑。此外，在带有更多训练时上下文的微调，可以提升有支持上下文条件下的精度，但测试时如缺乏或上下文信息混淆时鲁棒性变差。机制分析说明，长上下文让模型更依赖输入，而减少参数化知识。

Conclusion: 支持信息充裕悖论：无限扩展上下文窗口并非毫无代价，过多高质量上下文反而削弱模型本身知识内化能力。对于追求更强泛化与鲁棒性的模型，如何在内容内化与上下文利用之间权衡值得深思。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [155] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，将单一大语言模型（LLM）用于多智能体强化学习中的人机交互模拟，会导致策略泛化失效，主要因“模拟器塌缩”。作者提出推理时的Verbalized Sampling方法和训练时的Co-Training方法，有效缓解这一问题，并取得显著提升，最终开源了相关框架SCOPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体RL研究常以单个LLM模拟用户行为，然而单一模拟器的模式塌缩会让学习出的策略过拟合，难以迁移到其他用户或真实场景。作者希望系统分析并改善这一根本性问题，提升RL策略在真实环境的泛化能力。

Method: 1）理论上形式化并分析“模拟器塌缩”现象；2）提出推理时通过Verbalized Sampling，扩展模拟器响应分布以减少塌缩；3）提出训练时通过Co-Training联合多个可训练模拟器优化策略，防止策略过拟合单一模式。实验在三大多轮基准上验证两种方法的有效性。

Result: Verbalized Sampling方法相较单模拟器RL，提升了9%的泛化成功率；Co-Training进一步提升到14%。在真实用户测试中也获得相仿提升，两种方法均显著提升了策略多样性，防止策略和模拟器模式同时塌缩。

Conclusion: 多智能体RL中，提升训练环境的多样性对策略泛化至真实场景非常关键。作者提出的两种方法有效减少了模拟器塌缩，提升了RL范化能力，对行业和学术具有一定的启示意义，并开源了支持多模拟训练的SCOPE框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [156] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合无监督聚类与有监督分类的NLP混合模型，通过分析厄瓜多尔公共采购系统中投标人评论元数据，有效识别采购流程中的潜在违规行为，实现高效、低资源的风险预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购动用巨额资金，需持续审查以防止违规；但现有评论和公开数据未被充分利用。本研究试图开发低资源、高效方法，利用这些未充分挖掘的信息辅助风险识别。

Method: 提出了一个混合框架，先用Word2Vec、LLaMA、RoBERTa生成语义嵌入，无监督使用GMM聚类，随后用监督学习（随机森林）分类识别举报性质的评论。聚焦预合同阶段的公开评论分析。

Result: 实验显示：用领域内训练的Word2Vec嵌入、GMM聚类和随机森林分类器的组合，即使在类别极度不平衡时也能保持高精度和召回率。

Conclusion: 面向公共采购系统，结合轻量、领域自适应的NLP模型可以有效发现潜在风险，并提升系统透明度，无需大规模计算资源。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [157] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了AI教育工具面临的结构性障碍，尤其是孟加拉语等弱势语言在AI应用中的不平等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI被寄予厚望以缩小资源薄弱社区的教育和语言支持差距，但弱势语言使用者在AI工具背后的基础设施环节早已受到系统性的不利影响。

Method: 以孟加拉语为例，系统梳理训练语料、分词方案、评测基准和部署架构等环节中存在的结构性障碍，并通过统计数据量化了这些不足，如网络内容占比、训练语料数量、分词惩罚与网络接入率。

Result: 辨析出四大失败点：网页内容严重缺乏、训练语料极度不足、分词机制对孟加拉文的惩罚性效应、城乡网络接入鸿沟。

Conclusion: 数据稀缺应被视为结构性障碍，需要离线优先的基础设施设计来提升公平性，并呼吁语言学和AI领域推动消除这些不平等的研究方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为MiDAS的新方法，使机器人能通过极少量（如单次）示范与自主在线交互，实现高效任务适应和性能提升。该方法在多个实验环境中显著超过已有基线，并首次演示了从单次示范自适应出可靠机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器人自主学习由于稀疏奖励和弱探索能力难以从零成功学习新任务。作者希望探究如何最大限度减少人类干预，只用少量数据即可启动和引导机器人的自主学习，提升其实用性与泛化能力。

Method: 方法名为MiDAS，流程为：先基于少量（如1次）人类演示，用行为克隆（behavior cloning）微调预训练策略（VLA），再用基于价值的在线强化学习对策略残差进行优化。在LIBERO、RoboCasa等环境以及YAM双臂平台上进行了验证。

Result: MiDAS仅凭一次演示即可恢复甚至超过人类条件下的任务表现，且性能大幅超越其他基线方法。在YAM平台上，原本脆弱、成功率低的策略经过约6小时在线学习后，MiDAS能让其变得稳定且获得新技能。

Conclusion: MiDAS为极小样本机器人自适应学习提供了新的计算方案，实现了首次仅凭单次任务演示的可靠策略适应，为机器人自主学习和泛化能力提升提供了重要进展。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [159] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的交通场景生成框架TrafficDiffuser，能生成可解释的高层次交通场景，并提升仿真质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真器对初始状态要求高，限制了多样性和可扩展性。同时，数据驱动的初始化解释性差，轨迹生成难度大。

Method: 提出TrafficDiffuser模型，采用top-down策略，直接生成高层次交通场景（即初始-目标状态对），使初始化易于理解，并简化轨迹生成（视作插值问题）。

Result: 在Argoverse 2数据集上，TrafficDiffuser生成的初始状态贴近真实分布，并在速度分布距离和离开道路率上分别有55.3%和2.8%的改进，效果优于已有方法。

Conclusion: TrafficDiffuser提升了交通仿真的初始状态解释性和模拟真实性，为自动驾驶开发带来更可靠、可控的仿真平台。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [160] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一套全方位超声探头方向控制框架，实现了实时感知和保持任意探头角度，有效提升了超声成像的一致性和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超声成像依赖人工操作，图像质量受探头方向影响大，现有机器人系统主要支持垂直于体表的常规放置，无法满足如超声心动图等需非垂直角度检查的临床需求。

Method: 提出了一套融合RGB-D感知、本地表面建模和任务空间方向控制的框架，通过多视角点云融合生成局部表面模型，实现探头对任意预定方向的跟踪。

Result: 系统在平面表面跟踪、体模目标角度复现与活体内专家视野跟踪实验中，平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66度，最大可实现与表面法线夹角44.39±2.59度，成功获得所需心腔视图。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能实现高精度、全向的超声探头姿态控制，支持复杂临床场景，提升了机器人超声的实用价值。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [161] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在与机器人协作中，保留运动变异性（而非一味减少）如何影响用户体验，结果显示保留变异性可提升用户的自主感与实用性评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人辅助运动策略多假定人类运动是确定性的，并试图抑制运动变异性，但变异性可能对任务和体验有重要作用，相关设计缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者设计了一种支持自主动性的协同控制方法，用于保留用户自然的运动变异性。在一项用户实验中，参与者分别在无辅助、传统减少变异辅助、以及保留变异辅助三种模式下，操作智能轮椅完成任务，对比了三种模式下的运动变异性、任务表现和主观体验。

Result: 尽管各辅助模式下，任务表现相当，但采用保留自然运动变异性的策略时，参与者主观上报告了更高的自主感及有用性感受。

Conclusion: 保留人类运动变异性的辅助策略不仅能维持任务表现，还能增强用户的自主性与正面体验。助机器人系统应尊重、利用人类运动的内在结构，而非盲目视变异为需消除的噪声。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration. More broadly, the results highlight the importance of designing assistive robotic systems that respect the embodied structure of human movement rather than treating variability as noise to be neglected or eliminated.

</details>


### [162] [Herding End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11451)
*Simón Patiño Idarraga,Erick Silva,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经-符号安全守卫模块，用于在已有自动驾驶学习体系统令下发之前检查并修正违反安全规则的指令，提升行驶安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统虽有较高平均表现，但仍会犯低级交通违规错误，原因在于其仅学到统计模式、缺乏对物理安全条件的理解，无法保证安全且过程不透明。因此有必要设计结构性补充机制以保障安全。

Method: 提出在训练好的自动驾驶模型终端接口处加装神经-符号安全守卫，无需重新训练，依据显式安全规则，拦截、替换即将下发给车辆的违规指令，并保证每次干预都可溯源到具体规则，实现透明可控的安全保护。

Result: 在Fail2Drive与Bench2Drive等长尾基准上，以最先进的TransFuser v6模型为例，安全守卫使成功率提升15%，安全关键性碰撞减少最高至53%，同时原有驾驶评分不受影响。

Conclusion: 神经-符号安全守卫作为轻量级模块，能有效提升自动驾驶系统的安全和透明性，无需重新训练，有现实部署价值。

Abstract: Modern end-to-end driving agents can achieve high average performance yet still violate basic traffic rules that a human driver would never miss. The reason is structural: they learn statistical patterns rather than the physical conditions that guarantee safe driving, leaving their decision-making process opaque and safety constraints unenforced. We introduce a neuro-symbolic safety guard, a lightweight module that attaches to the final command interface of an already-trained agent. Immediately before a command reaches the vehicle, it checks the command against explicit safety rules and, only when necessary, replaces it with the nearest safe alternative. Each intervention is directly executable and traceable to the rule that triggered it, while the guard itself requires no retraining and adds no learned component. Evaluated on the long-tail benchmarks Fail2Drive and Bench2Drive using the state-of-the-art TransFuser v6 (TFv6) as a case study, the guard improves Success Rate by 15% and reduces safety-critical collisions by up to 53%, while preserving the original Driving Score.

</details>


### [163] [Koopman Representation of Nonlinear Virtual Environments in Kinesthetic Haptic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11461)
*Yanting Zhou,Jozsef Kövecses,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Koopman算子来建模非线性虚拟环境下的力反馈系统，并通过仿真、实验和用户研究验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于被动性的稳定性分析方法对非线性虚拟环境过于保守，且在存在模型不确定性时表现不佳，因此需要更准确和鲁棒的方法进行非线性虚拟环境的建模与稳定性分析。

Method: 利用Koopman算子，将原本的非线性虚拟环境动力学提升到更高维线性感知下表示，使其能用线性组合描述非线性系统。通过仿真、实验以及多用户研究验证这种表示方法在Duffing振子场景下的有效性，并基于此进行闭环（CL）稳定性分析。

Result: 实验和仿真结果表明，所提方法可以有效准确地表示和反馈Duffing-oscillator等非线性VE的动力学，并且用户研究也验证了方法的优越性。此外，基于Koopman方法的稳定性分析不如传统方法保守，对模型不确定性更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的非线性虚拟环境建模和分析方法不仅能更好地进行闭环力反馈系统的稳定性判据，还能提升建模的鲁棒性，是代替传统非线性建模与分析的方法之一。

Abstract: Rendering haptic feedback with nonlinear virtual environments (VEs) is important in many applications that require highly accurate force feedback. This paper considers the use of the Koopman operator to represent a nonlinear VE interacting with a haptic system. Simulation and experimental results demonstrated that the proposed method provides an effective representation of the nonlinear dynamics of a Duffing-oscillator VE. A multi-user study further confirmed this conclusion. In addition, a closed-loop (CL) stability analysis is performed leveraging the Koopman representation of the nonlinear VE to access stability of the overall haptic system. This alternative way of representing nonlinear VEs enables a convenient CL stability analysis that is less conservative than traditional passivity-based methods. Since a linear combination of all lifted states is used to represent the nonlinearity, such representation is also more robust to uncertainties in the modeling of the haptic device than a traditional nonlinear model.

</details>


### [164] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了当前机器人系统WAMs对未来轨迹展开（rollout）的依赖，并提出了一种无rollout的新方法RIFT，可大幅减少部署延迟，同时保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs模型通过迭代的视频rollout预测未来，尽管可提升行为准确性，却极大增加了系统延迟。作者疑问：是否真的需要完整的未来轨迹，还是仅需其未来特征表示？若突破rollout依赖，将大幅提升机器人实时决策能力。

Method: 作者在LIBERO数据集的40个任务及RoboTwin 2.0环境下，分析了四种主流WAMs模型对未来缓存（future-cache）的敏感性与依赖性。进一步提出RIFT方法，用学习的anticipation tokens单步生成完整未来K/V cache，无需迭代rollout，且接口兼容原WAM。

Result: RIFT在LIBERO上达98.8%成功率，接近主流rollout方法，但将延迟降低68.2%~89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0新场景中，RIFT也刷新了最高成功率（92.9/92.6%）。同时实验验证部分WAMs模型能够复用固定cache，指示存在无需完整rollout的可能性。

Conclusion: 本文证实，有些WAMs模型对未来缓存的生成与消费可解耦，通过RIFT可实现不依赖rollout的高性能未来条件化，显著提升机器人部署效率。这一成果为机器人智能决策的实时性及可扩展性带来新方向。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [165] [RoadWeaver: Large-Scale Lane-Level HD Map Generation from Scratch for Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11580)
*Yueyuan Li,Zexi Chen,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RoadWeaver的新方法，从零生成多样且大规模的高精地图，用于支持自动驾驶仿真，并优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶仿真对多样且可扩展的车道级高精地图需求很大。现有方法要么依赖手工制作或真实地图，难以扩展，要么只能生成局部路网结构，无法获得完整高精地图。打造能自动生成完整、多样且大规模高精地图的新方法至关重要。

Method: RoadWeaver采用粗到细的框架。首先合成全球路网布局，再扩展至连通的真实路网，最终生成车道级的几何结构，保证拓扑一致的车道连接。所有步骤自动化、从零生成地图。

Result: 实验证明，RoadWeaver可实现99.8%的可达性、10.7%的死胡同比例和0.24米的端点对齐误差。端点对齐误差比现有最优方法降低94.4%，且可用时为1.39-3.50秒，即时生成完整地图。

Conclusion: RoadWeaver能高效生成可直接用于驾驶仿真的大规模、多样车道级高精地图，为后续自动驾驶系统的闭环测试提供可扩展的仿真环境。代码和实现将开放。

Abstract: Autonomous driving simulation requires diverse and scalable lane-level HD maps to support long-horizon evaluation across complex road networks. Existing approaches either rely on handcrafted or reconstructed real-world maps, which limits scalability, or generate only local road structures rather than complete HD maps. We present RoadWeaver, a coarse-to-fine framework for from-scratch generation of diverse, large-scale HD maps. RoadWeaver first synthesizes a global road layout, expands it into a connected road network, and then constructs lane-level geometry with topologically consistent lane connectivity. Experimental results show that RoadWeaver achieves a 99.8\% reachability, a 10.7\% dead-end ratio, and an endpoint alignment error of 0.24 m. Compared with SOTA generation methods, it reduces endpoint alignment error by 94.4\% while generating complete HD maps in 1.39--3.50 s. The generated maps can be directly deployed in driving simulators, providing scalable simulation environments for future closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems. The training code and an out-of-the-box implementation of RoadWeaver will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [166] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian,Xinrui Zhang,Tianyu Li,Peizhi Zhang,Guirong Zhou,Haojie Feng,Junpeng Huang,Qixiang Zhang,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Video2Track框架，将真实道路视频中的交互驾驶场景迁移到可控的封闭测试场地，实现更加真实、多样和可调风险的自动驾驶系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有封闭测试方法多依赖标准化协议或预设轨迹，场景交互过于刻板，难以复现公共道路交互的复杂性。需要能从真实场景迁移、更具自然复杂性的测试方法。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：1）场景语义映射，通过视觉-语言模型从视频提取语义并检索到封闭场地图书馆，确定场景片段与交互锚点；2）动态交互测试，基于上述锚点，利用条件扩散模型生成多智能体轨迹，并基于Stackelberg博弈调节交互强度，达成参数化的对抗测试。

Result: 实验证明框架能真实还原现实中的交互场景，并能生成多样、可控风险等级和不同交互风格的可执行场景变体，优于以往刻板的预设测试。

Conclusion: Video2Track为自动驾驶系统提供了一种可扩展、真实且可操控的封闭场地验证新方式，有助于提升安全关键场景的测试覆盖与效力。

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>


### [167] [IoT-Enabled Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Smart Ports: A Curriculum-Guided Shared Policy Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11597)
*Yuqing Lin,Rangya Zhang,Kum Fai Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于课程引导的强化学习框架，提高了物联网自主航运设备在复杂港口环境中的导航可靠性和扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能港口对物联网自主航运设备的依赖增强，如何保证这些设备在交通密集和信息不完全环境下的导航智能与运行安全成为关键挑战。

Method: 作者设计了一个课程引导的强化学习体系，采用带有共享递归策略的结构来加强时间推理能力，并通过集中式离线训练和边缘智能决策实现端到端导航。同时，方法在多种逼真港口环境中利用仿真评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提方法在导航可靠性、避碰能力以及训练稳定性等方面，相比标准基线法均有显著提升，并能很好地泛化到未见过的高密度场景。

Conclusion: 课程引导共享学习方法为大规模部署物联网自主航运设备提供了切实可行的智能航运解决方案，特别在智能港口应用场景下表现突出。

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>


### [168] [StellaVLA: In-Context Structured Demonstration for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11671)
*Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Zijian Wang,Dihao Zhu,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Daochang Liu,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StellaVLA框架通过条件化结构化演示帮助视觉-语言-动作模型在新环境下无需微调自适应，实现跨体态任务迁移，超越现有模型，在各项基准测试中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的VLA模型在遇到不同于训练时的场景、视角或物体时表现大幅下降，现有方法需要额外数据和微调才能适应新情况，效率低下。因此，亟需一种无需额外标注、能高效适应分布外新任务的方法。

Method: 提出StellaVLA框架，在测试时通过检索到的单次结构化演示进行条件化自适应。利用自动化离线流程将原始操作轨迹转为结构化演示（如任务计划、子目标描述和三维动作表述），不需人工标注。训练时采用双通道联合动作和语言目标训练，推理时用动作专家保证实时高频控制。

Result: 在VLA-Arena排行榜上StellaVLA得分0.63，明显优于强基线($π_{0.5}$ 0.44, LingBot-VLA 0.22)；在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上成功率分别为98.8%和85.1%。实机器人验证表明框架能利用不同体态的人类、机器人及XR演示完成任务适应。

Conclusion: StellaVLA实现了无需微调的分布外自适应和体态迁移，显著提升VLA模型在多样任务场景下的泛化能力，为提升智能体现实适应性提供新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>


### [169] [ContactIPM: A Structure-Exploiting Interior-Point Solver for Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11731)
*Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的隐式接触轨迹优化方法ContactIPM，结合了舞台化最优控制分解和原-对偶一致性，在多个基准任务中表现出比现有方法更快和更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式接触轨迹优化可避免预设接触序列，但包含互补约束使得常规求解器难以高效、稳健地解决，现有专用方法难以兼顾结构分解与原-对偶一致性。作者希望弥补这两个方向的不足，提出一种同时结合二者优点的新方法。

Method: 提出了ContactIPM方法，识别互补不等式对，通过耦合弹性内点松弛进行嵌入，分阶段消除松变量和对偶变量，并利用Riccati递归求解简化后的牛顿系统。此外采用多阶段MPCC恢复策略，设有续算和重启机制，终止由未松弛的物理互补残差把控。

Result: 在与CRISP和IMPACT两种接触MPCC方法的对比实验中，ContactIPM在4个固定基准下平均快2.17到8.87倍，在Push Box和Push-T健壮性任务中成功率更高。对比IMPACT时在两个任务上快2.96和4.91倍，在Push Box上慢4.46倍。在50次闭环Push Box实验中也针对模型失配和噪声等进行了评测。

Conclusion: ContactIPM有效结合了接触约束处理的结构化分解和原-对偶算法优势，显著提升了求解效率和成功率，为隐式接触轨迹优化问题提供了更可靠和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization avoids prescribing contact sequences, but yields mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) whose degeneracy challenges conventional primal--dual solvers. Existing contact-specific methods improve robustness to this degeneracy but do not leverage a stagewise optimal-control factorization and primal--dual consistency, while structure-exploiting optimal-control solvers are not designed for complementarity constraints. We show that these capabilities can be combined in a single primal--dual method. ContactIPM identifies complementary inequality pairs, embeds them through a barrier-coupled elastic interior relaxation, eliminates slack and dual variables stagewise, and solves the reduced Newton system using a Riccati recursion. A fixed multi-phase MPCC recovery schedule provides four continuation and restart attempts from naive initializations, while termination is gated by the unrelaxed physical complementarity residual.
  We compare ContactIPM with two contact-specific MPCC solvers, CRISP and IMPACT, using matched benchmark conditions and common post-solve acceptance criteria. On four fixed CRISP benchmark cases, ContactIPM is $2.17$--$8.87\times$ faster over 20 paired timing repetitions per case and achieves higher success on the Push Box and Push-T robustness suites. Against IMPACT, ContactIPM is \(2.96\times\) faster on Push T and \(4.91\times\) faster on Cart Transport, but \(4.46\times\) slower on Push Box. In 50 closed-loop Push Box rollouts spanning model mismatch, measurement noise, initial-pose errors, and state resets,

</details>


### [170] [G0.5: One Autoregressive Stream for Robot Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11739)
*Yicheng Liu,Zibin Dong,Baijun Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Tao Jiang,Anqi Yang,Shicheng Cao,Haonan Liu,Yue Sun,Zihan Guo,Xiao Liu,Dong Ke,Changxun Pan,Chenru Wu,Tailai Cheng,Xiaoshu Ren,Xinlei Zhang,Jianning Cui,Zijie Zhao,Haoyu Zhang,Kaiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Bowen Zhang,Jiahui Niu,Shaoting Zhu,Shiduo Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了G0.5，一种端到端自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基础模型，实现了图像理解、语言推理与多机器人动作生成的统一，显著超过现有多项标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型常将视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为上下文编码器，而不是决策器，无法实现推理与动作生成的全面融合。为解决此瓶颈，作者希望构建能原生融合推理和动作输出的统一模型，充分释放VLM对物理行为的迁移能力。

Method: G0.5模型采取单一自回归Transformer解码器，同时输出推理与动作，将机器人异构动作映射为共享词汇表，引入链式推理流与视觉记忆模块，与大规模机器人、VQA数据集联合预训练。

Result: G0.5在7个交叉领域的评测中整体超过SOTA模型，包括实机精调、长期任务挑战赛、零样本迁移仿真、指令执行、物体搬运等，提升幅度显著。

Conclusion: G0.5首次实现了推理-动作一体的基础VLA模型，显著提升多机器人多任务泛化与任务指令理解能力，对未来端到端机器人智能基础模型具有重要推动作用。

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>


### [171] [Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11769)
*Chaeyeon Jung,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在双臂人形机器人操作任务中的初始姿态依赖性。发现初始姿态与策略之间存在强烈交互，导致手选择、成功率等大幅变化。提出了量化手偏好与手选择表现的指标，并探讨了改进策略鲁棒性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际机器人任务中，VLA策略需要适应各种初始配置，但总体任务成功率往往掩盖了姿态相关的失败与不当的手选择。因此，研究初始姿态对策略表现的影响，有助于提升机器人操作的稳健性和可靠性。

Method: 通过实验对比多种VLA策略和17种初始臂部配置，使用HandPriorScore等指标分析早期手选择偏好及其与成功率的关系。测试不同数据集覆盖度、训练配置和观察方式下的策略表现，并评估数据增强和构成对稳健性的影响。

Result: 实验显示，不同策略在相同初始姿态下成功率差别明显，同一策略不同姿态下表现迥异。初始臂姿可以显著影响手选择倾向，且这种偏好会被训练数据和观察类型所调节。增加训练中初始姿态的多样性能有效改善策略的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了初始姿态条件下的手偏好机制，确定了某些姿态配置作为手选择行为调节的关键因子。数据覆盖范围和训练集构成对策略鲁棒性影响显著，为提升VLA策略泛化能力提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

</details>


### [172] [Enhancing Visual Domain Robustness in Behaviour Cloning via Saliency-Guided Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11870)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nils Ingelhag,Ville Kyrki,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的显著性引导数据增强方法（RoboSaGA），用于提升视觉为基础的行为克隆（BC）在遇到严重视觉域偏移（如阴影、干扰物、背景变化）时的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像增强方法（如裁剪、色彩扰动）在大幅视觉变化下表现有限，叠加式增强虽在计算机视觉有效，但用于行为克隆时有语义信息丢失风险，因此亟需可兼顾泛化与任务语义保留的新方法。

Method: RoboSaGA通过策略驱动的显著性图，在像素级动态调整增强强度，对任务无关区域进行强增强，同时保护任务关键区域信息，无需结构改动或额外损失，可直接整合进现有架构。

Result: RoboSaGA在仿真和真实环境中实验，显著提升了对视觉域偏移（如干扰物、背景、光照、阴影变化）的鲁棒性，同时保持在原域的表现。

Conclusion: RoboSaGA为视觉行为克隆提供了兼顾语义保留和泛化的增强方案，无需额外复杂度，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: In vision-based behavior cloning (BC), conventional image augmentations such as Random Crop and Color Jitter often fall short under substantial visual domain shifts, including changes in shadows, distractors, and backgrounds. Superimposition-based augmentations, which blend in-domain and out-of-domain images, have shown promise for improving generalization in computer vision, but their suitability for BC remains uncertain because task-critical semantics, spatiotemporal relationships, and agent-target interactions must be preserved. To address this, we introduce RoboSaGA, a Saliency-Guided Augmentation method within the superimposition family tailored for vision-based BC. RoboSaGA dynamically adjusts augmentation intensity at the pixel level using policy-driven saliency, enabling aggressive augmentation in task-irrelevant regions while preserving task-critical information. It integrates seamlessly into existing architectures without requiring structural modifications or additional learning objectives. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that RoboSaGA preserves in-domain performance while substantially improving robustness to visual domain shifts, including distractor and background changes, as well as lighting and shadow variations. Code is available at https://github.com/Zheyu-Zhuang/RoboSaGA.

</details>


### [173] [D3D-GEN: Robot-Aware Domain-Grounded Interactive 3D World Generation for Social Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11876)
*Anh Duc Do,Volodymyr Scherbyna,Tai Duc Nguyen,Spaarsh Thakkar,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Archan Misra,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了D3D-GEN系统，可以快速自动化生成逼真的3D社会导航仿真环境，支持多领域、可扩展，并支持主流仿真平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Embodied AI社会导航仿真环境很难兼顾真实性和可仿真性，缺乏灵活高效的3D场景生成方法。

Method: 提出D3D-GEN系统，结合领域智能体和基于检索增强生成（RAG）的管线。系统从领域描述出发，自动收集公开数据并建立数据库，然后通过RAG动态生成合理的平面图和物体布局，生成3D互动场景，支持主流仿真器。

Result: 该系统可快速生成住宅、医院、办公室等多种场景的数据库，并为每个领域生成数十个不同且合理的仿真环境，已集成本地网页前端。

Conclusion: D3D-GEN显著提升了3D仿真世界的生成效率和灵活性，有助于社会导航和机器人仿真的研究发展。

Abstract: Training and validation of Embodied AI for social navigation critically depends on realistic simulation environments, yet many current approaches fail to find a balance between realism and simulability. We propose D3D-GEN, a novel world generation system that combines a domain agent with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in that domain. Our system enables users to rapidly generate domain-grounded, fully interactive 3D worlds by automating both the collection of domain knowledge and the synthesis of realistic floorplans and object placements, without dependence on any fixed 3D model database. Given a domain description prompt, the research agent collects publicly accessible domain-specific data and constructs a persistent domain database. Using this database, our RAG pipeline generates plausible floorplans and object placements by dynamically querying a user-provided semantic database, which can be easily extended or modified. The output is a fully interactive 3D world loadable by the popular simulators Isaac Sim and Gazebo. With our approach, we have built databases for several common domains (indoor residential, hospital, office) and generated dozens of distinct, plausible simulation environments for each domain. We present D3D-GEN with a local web frontend that facilitates rapid, interactive world generation for robot simulation.

</details>


### [174] [Scalable Multi-Agent Maze Traversal with Local Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11895)
*Julian Rau,Jahir Argote-Gerald,Grace McFassel,Genki Miyauchi,Paul Trodden,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式算法，允许多智能体在未知且通信受限的迷宫状环境中集体导航、探索并归集至目标点，有效避免碰撞并优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂如洞穴网络、管道系统等环境中，多智能体需要高效探索和导航，但受限于未知拓扑和有限通信，传统方法难以应用。

Method: 提出了一种分布式算法，智能体依次进入起点，并通过局部通信建立领队-跟随关系及动态切换领队。每次仅有一名智能体进行探索，利用单体智能体的迷宫求解器。算法具备完备性，并推导了其时间与空间复杂度。

Result: 模拟实验（多达625智能体）显示平均总“燃料”消耗随智能体数量增加而降低，且新算法性能明显优于所有智能体独立执行单一迷宫解算器的基线。

Conclusion: 所提分布式算法实现在未知环境中多智能体高效、安全协作导航，具备优良扩展性与资源使用效率，为类似实际应用提供了理论和实验基础。

Abstract: Cave networks, pipe systems, and similar maze-like environments pose significant challenges for multi-agent navigation in unknown settings with limited communication. We propose a distributed algorithm that enables agents to collectively traverse an unknown, possibly cyclic graph. Agents enter sequentially at a designated start node and are tasked to localize and reach an undisclosed goal while avoiding collisions. They coordinate via local communication using leader-follower relationships and leader switching. At any moment in time, exploration is performed by only one of the agents, which runs a single-agent maze solver. We prove that the algorithm is complete, that its makespan is asymptotically equivalent (in the number of agents) to that of an optimal full-knowledge strategy, and derive its time and space complexity. Simulations with up to $625$ agents show a decreasing average sum-of-fuels as the number of agents increases and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms a naïve baseline in which all agents independently execute the single-agent solver.

</details>


### [175] [DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11901)
*Zihao Xie,Pingrui Lai,Yitong Wu,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DaViNCi数据集，首个同时引入连续动作和动态环境的室外视觉-语言导航(VLN)任务，极大提升了任务的现实性与挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外VLN数据集基于离散拓扑图，难以反映现实环境的连续与动态变化，影响了从模拟到现实的迁移能力。

Method: 作者提出DaViNCi数据集，允许智能体在连续空间中采取连续动作，并需应对不可预测的动态因素。数据集覆盖六种地图，包含6,933条导航轨迹，并通过与现有数据集的实验性对比分析。

Result: 在DaViNCi数据集上的导航成功率相比传统离散环境下降超过10%；在连续环境下表现下降更明显，验证了任务的挑战性。

Conclusion: DaViNCi推动了VLN在更真实室外环境下的研究，其引入的连续性与动态性对研究具有重要意义，并揭示了动作粒度和动态因素对任务表现的影响。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has progressively expanded from indoor to outdoor environments. However, existing outdoor VLN datasets still rely on fixed discrete topological graphs for construction. It fails to align with the rapidly changing real-world outdoor environments and impedes the sim-to-real transfer of VLN agents. To address this limitation, we propose DaViNCi (\textbf{D}yn\textbf{a}mic \textbf{Vi}sion-and-Language \textbf{N}avigation in \textbf{C}ont\textbf{i}nuous Environment), the first outdoor VLN dataset that simultaneously introduces both continuous and dynamic factors. The agent not only moves in the outdoor environment using continuous actions but is also required to handle unpredictable dynamic elements. The dataset encompasses six distinct maps with a total of 6,933 trajectories. Through comprehensive comparative experiments, we find that the success rate on DaViNCi decreased by more than 10\% in discrete environments compared to previous datasets. And there is an even greater decline in continuous settings, demonstrating the challenge of DaViNCi. Furthermore, we clarify the impact of action granularity and dynamic elements. These results demonstrate the practical value of DaViNCi in advancing outdoor VLN toward more realistic environments. The website is https://xzh0312.github.io/DaViNCi/.

</details>


### [176] [Learning Loco-Manipulation From SMPC Demonstrations With Sparse Offline-to-Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12063)
*Martin Schuck,Maks Sorokin,Simone Manni,Duy Ta,Angela P. Schoellig,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'H,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种结合采样型模型预测控制（SMPC）与离线强化学习的新方法，用于高效训练机器人自主的运动与操作技能，并实现了从仿真到现实的成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人自主性的提升受限于强化学习中密集奖励设计过程的繁琐与效率低下。该研究旨在寻找无需大量人工调参，也能高效解决复杂运动与操作任务的新途径。

Method: 作者在仿真环境中采用SMPC自动生成优质演示数据，离线提供给强化学习智能体，以稀疏任务奖励监督训练，极大改善探索效率。整体方案还结合了底层动态稳定性控制器。最终通过端到端的sim-to-real方法在不同机器人形态（如带机械臂的Spot四足机器人和G1类人机器人）上进行了验证。

Result: 提出的框架显著减少了学习新技能所需时间，无需人工调整奖励函数。训练出的策略在执行任务时甚至超越了原始的最优控制专家，在多种机器人平台上展现出复杂技能的鲁棒性和迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过自动化专家演示与稀疏奖励离线强化学习的整合，能够高效训练机器人复杂技能，实现仿真到现实的顺利迁移，并且简化了手动奖励设计流程，推进了机器人自主性的发展。

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [177] [HandEdit: A Unified Benchmark for Egocentric Human-to-Robot Dexterous Hand Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12122)
*Zhenjie Yang,Xingyu Jiao,Guopeng Zhong,Shuzhe Yang,Shi Che,Chao Wu,Chenyu Jiang,Dongjie Zhang,Yideng Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Haisheng Su,Shuang Jin,Donghang Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HandEdit，这是一个专门用于将人类手部/手臂在第一视角图像中转化为多样化机器人义肢的，具有大规模标注的图像编辑数据集和基准。该数据集和评测体系为人-机数据协同训练和具身AI发展提供重要资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集真实机器人灵巧操作的数据代价高昂，而人类手部第一视角视频数据丰富，但人与机器人在外观、动作和视角上的差异严重阻碍了协同训练。现有通用图像编辑模型无法充分弥合这种差距，因此亟需专门针对具身差异的数据资源和评测。

Method: 作者提出HandEdit，一个拥有逾2亿标注编辑实例的数据集，涵盖26种不同的机器手URDF，包括手型和手臂两类，并建立了统一评测协议。评测方案涵盖手型与手臂两条赛道，支持URDF条件评测，并选取了11种代表性图像编辑基线模型在多维指标（通用相似度、VLM打分和具身感知）下进行评测。

Result: 实验系统性评估了各种基线方案，利用HandEdit揭示了当前方法在具身编辑上的优势和不足，为后续模型开发和评测提供了权威基准。HandEdit数据集极大丰富了人体到机器手的转换场景，有力促进了具身感知图像编辑模型和下游机器手学习的可扩展性。

Conclusion: HandEdit为图像编辑和机器人领域融合提供了重要数据基础，有助于推动更通用、可扩展的具身式AI发展，使机器人能够基于丰富的人类视频数据自主学习抓取与操作。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>


### [178] [Learning-Based Behavior Planning for Automated Driving: Real-World Integration and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12198)
*Jean-Pierre Busch,Guido Linden,Jan Bergmann,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习与传统规划方法的自动驾驶混合规划架构，既利用了神经网络在复杂场景下的学习能力，又保障了安全与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和深度学习方法在自动驾驶中的运动规划表现出巨大潜力，但其复杂性、不可解释性和可信度问题影响了安全保证。为了应对这些挑战，作者提出混合架构以提升可验证性和安全性。

Method: 作者设计了由深度神经网络负责在复杂交通场景下输出驾驶行为建议，然后采用基于优化的监督层对神经网络的建议进行验证，强制安全和可行性约束。

Result: 通过在真实城市道路数据上的开放环评测，验证了所提出架构的驾驶行为表现；同时讨论了系统集成和闭环运行的稳定性，并在实际车辆上完成了部署和结果报告。

Conclusion: 混合规划架构能够结合神经网络的学习优势和传统方法的安全可验证特性，为提升自动驾驶系统的透明度和可靠性提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

</details>
